It includes a working example with frontend rendering, live context updates, and RPC communication to a backend server.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://cloudcli.ai/docs/plugin-overview\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ePlugin Documentation →\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — full guide to the plugin API, manifest format, security model, and more.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eFAQ\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-faq\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: FAQ\" href=\"#faq\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003eHow is this different from Claude Code Remote Control?\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eClaude Code Remote Control lets you send messages to a session already running in your local terminal. Your machine has to stay on, your terminal has to stay open, and sessions time out after roughly 10 minutes without a network connection.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eCloudCLI UI and CloudCLI Cloud extend Claude Code rather than sit alongside it — your MCP servers, permissions, settings, and sessions are the exact same ones Claude Code uses natively. Nothing is duplicated or managed separately.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eHere's what that means in practice:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAll your sessions, not just one\u003c/strong\u003e — CloudCLI UI auto-discovers every session from your \u003ccode\u003e~/.claude\u003c/code\u003e folder. Remote Control only exposes the single active session to make it available in the Claude mobile app.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eYour settings are your settings\u003c/strong\u003e — MCP servers, tool permissions, and project config you change in CloudCLI UI are written directly to your Claude Code config and take effect immediately, and vice versa.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWorks with more agents\u003c/strong\u003e — Claude Code, Cursor CLI, Codex, and Gemini CLI, not just Claude Code.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFull UI, not just a chat window\u003c/strong\u003e — file explorer, Git integration, MCP management, and a shell terminal are all built in.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCloudCLI Cloud runs in the cloud\u003c/strong\u003e — close your laptop, the agent keeps running. No terminal to babysit, no machine to keep awake.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003eDo I need to pay for an AI subscription separately?\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYes. CloudCLI provides the environment, not the AI. You bring your own Claude, Cursor, Codex, or Gemini subscription. CloudCLI Cloud starts at $7/month for the hosted environment on top of that.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003eCan I use CloudCLI UI on my phone?\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYes. For self-hosted, run the server on your machine and open \u003ccode\u003e[yourip]:port\u003c/code\u003e in any browser on your network. For CloudCLI Cloud, open it from any device — no VPN, no port forwarding, no setup. A native app is also in the works.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003eWill changes I make in the UI affect my local Claude Code setup?\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYes, for self-hosted. CloudCLI UI reads from and writes to the same \u003ccode\u003e~/.claude\u003c/code\u003e config that Claude Code uses natively. MCP servers you add via the UI show up in Claude Code immediately and vice versa.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2   <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Cloud CLI (aka Claude Code UI)</h1><a id="user-content-cloud-cli-aka-claude-code-ui" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Cloud CLI (aka Claude Code UI)" href="#cloud-cli-aka-claude-code-ui"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
  <p dir="auto">A desktop and mobile UI for <a href="https://docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/claude-code" rel="nofollow">Claude Code</a>, <a href="https://docs.cursor.com/en/cli/overview" rel="nofollow">Cursor CLI</a>, <a href="https://developers.openai.com/codex" rel="nofollow">Codex</a>, and <a href="https://geminicli.com/" rel="nofollow">Gemini-CLI</a>.<br>Use it locally or remotely to view your active projects and sessions from everywhere.</p>
</div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Features</h2><a id="user-content-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Features" href="#features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Responsive Design</strong> - Works seamlessly across desktop, tablet, and mobile so you can also use Agents from mobile</li>
<li><strong>Interactive Chat Interface</strong> - Built-in chat interface for seamless communication with the Agents</li>
<li><strong>Integrated Shell Terminal</strong> - Direct access to the Agents CLI through built-in shell functionality</li>
<li><strong>File Explorer</strong> - Interactive file tree with syntax highlighting and live editing</li>
<li><strong>Git Explorer</strong> - View, stage and commit your changes. You can also switch branches</li>
<li><strong>Session Management</strong> - Resume conversations, manage multiple sessions, and track history</li>
<li><strong>Plugin System</strong> - Extend CloudCLI with custom plugins — add new tabs, backend services, and integrations. <a href="https://github.com/cloudcli-ai/cloudcli-plugin-starter">Build your own →</a></li>
<li><strong>TaskMaster AI Integration</strong> <em>(Optional)</em> - Advanced project management with AI-powered task planning, PRD parsing, and workflow automation</li>
<li><strong>Model Compatibility</strong> - Works with Claude, GPT, and Gemini model families (the full list of supported models is available at runtime via <code>GET /api/providers/:provider/models</code>)</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CloudCLI Cloud (Recommended)</h3><a id="user-content-cloudcli-cloud-recommended" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CloudCLI Cloud (Recommended)" href="#cloudcli-cloud-recommended"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The fastest way to get started — no local setup required. Get a fully managed, containerized development environment accessible from the web, mobile app, API, or your favorite IDE.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong><a href="https://cloudcli.ai" rel="nofollow">Get started with CloudCLI Cloud</a></strong></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Self-Hosted (Open source)</h3><a id="user-content-self-hosted-open-source" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Self-Hosted (Open source)" href="#self-hosted-open-source"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">npm</h4><a id="user-content-npm" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: npm" href="#npm"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Try CloudCLI UI instantly with <strong>npx</strong> (requires <strong>Node.js</strong> v22+):</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npx @cloudcli-ai/cloudcli"><pre class="notranslate"><code>npx @cloudcli-ai/cloudcli
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Or install <strong>globally</strong> for regular use:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install -g @cloudcli-ai/cloudcli
cloudcli"><pre class="notranslate"><code>npm install -g @cloudcli-ai/cloudcli
cloudcli
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Open <code>http://localhost:3001</code> — all your existing sessions are discovered automatically.</p>
<p dir="auto">Visit the <strong><a href="https://cloudcli.ai/docs" rel="nofollow">documentation →</a></strong> for full configuration options, PM2, remote server setup and more.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Docker Sandboxes (Experimental)</h4><a id="user-content-docker-sandboxes-experimental" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Docker Sandboxes (Experimental)" href="#docker-sandboxes-experimental"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Run agents in isolated sandboxes with hypervisor-level isolation. Starts Claude Code by default. Requires the <a href="https://docs.docker.com/ai/sandboxes/get-started/" rel="nofollow"><code>sbx</code> CLI</a>.</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npx @cloudcli-ai/cloudcli@latest sandbox ~/my-project"><pre class="notranslate"><code>npx @cloudcli-ai/cloudcli@latest sandbox ~/my-project
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Supports Claude Code, Codex, and Gemini CLI. See the <a href="/siteboon/claudecodeui/blob/main/docker">sandbox docs</a> for setup and advanced options.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Which option is right for you?</h2><a id="user-content-which-option-is-right-for-you" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Which option is right for you?" href="#which-option-is-right-for-you"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">CloudCLI UI is the open source UI layer that powers CloudCLI Cloud. You can self-host it on your own machine, run it in a Docker sandbox for isolation, or use CloudCLI Cloud for a fully managed environment.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Security &amp; Tools Configuration</h2><a id="user-content-security--tools-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security &amp; Tools Configuration" href="#security--tools-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>🔒 Important Notice</strong>: All Claude Code tools are <strong>disabled by default</strong>. This prevents potentially harmful operations from running automatically.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Enabling Tools</h3><a id="user-content-enabling-tools" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Enabling Tools" href="#enabling-tools"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To use Claude Code's full functionality, you'll need to manually enable tools:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Open Tools Settings</strong> - Click the gear icon in the sidebar</li>
<li><strong>Enable Selectively</strong> - Turn on only the tools you need</li>
<li><strong>Apply Settings</strong> - Your preferences are saved locally</li>
</ol>
<div align="center" dir="auto">
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/siteboon/claudecodeui/blob/main/public/screenshots/tools-modal.png"><img src="/siteboon/claudecodeui/raw/main/public/screenshots/tools-modal.png" alt="Tools Settings Modal" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<em>Tools Settings interface - enable only what you need</em></p>
</div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Recommended approach</strong>: Start with basic tools enabled and add more as needed. You can always adjust these settings later.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Plugins</h2><a id="user-content-plugins" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Plugins" href="#plugins"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">CloudCLI has a plugin system that lets you add custom tabs with their own frontend UI and optional Node.js backend. Install plugins from git repos directly in <strong>Settings &gt; Plugins</strong>, or build your own.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Available Plugins</h3><a id="user-content-available-plugins" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Available Plugins" href="#available-plugins"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Plugin</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong><a href="https://github.com/cloudcli-ai/cloudcli-plugin-starter">Project Stats</a></strong></td>
<td>Shows file counts, lines of code, file-type breakdown, largest files, and recently modified files for your current project</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong><a href="https://github.com/cloudcli-ai/cloudcli-plugin-terminal">Web Terminal</a></strong></td>
<td>Full xterm.js terminal with multi-tab support</td>
</tr>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Build Your Own</h3><a id="user-content-build-your-own" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Build Your Own" href="#build-your-own"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong><a href="https://github.com/cloudcli-ai/cloudcli-plugin-starter">Plugin Starter Template →</a></strong> — fork this repo to create your own plugin. It includes a working example with frontend rendering, live context updates, and RPC communication to a backend server.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong><a href="https://cloudcli.ai/docs/plugin-overview" rel="nofollow">Plugin Documentation →</a></strong> — full guide to the plugin API, manifest format, security model, and more.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">FAQ</h2><a id="user-content-faq" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: FAQ" href="#faq"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<details>
<summary>How is this different from Claude Code Remote Control?</summary>
<p dir="auto">Claude Code Remote Control lets you send messages to a session already running in your local terminal. Your machine has to stay on, your terminal has to stay open, and sessions time out after roughly 10 minutes without a network connection.</p>
<p dir="auto">CloudCLI UI and CloudCLI Cloud extend Claude Code rather than sit alongside it — your MCP servers, permissions, settings, and sessions are the exact same ones Claude Code uses natively. Nothing is duplicated or managed separately.</p>
<p dir="auto">Here's what that means in practice:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>All your sessions, not just one</strong> — CloudCLI UI auto-discovers every session from your <code>~/.claude</code> folder. Remote Control only exposes the single active session to make it available in the Claude mobile app.</li>
<li><strong>Your settings are your settings</strong> — MCP servers, tool permissions, and project config you change in CloudCLI UI are written directly to your Claude Code config and take effect immediately, and vice versa.</li>
<li><strong>Works with more agents</strong> — Claude Code, Cursor CLI, Codex, and Gemini CLI, not just Claude Code.</li>
<li><strong>Full UI, not just a chat window</strong> — file explorer, Git integration, MCP management, and a shell terminal are all built in.</li>
<li><strong>CloudCLI Cloud runs in the cloud</strong> — close your laptop, the agent keeps running. No terminal to babysit, no machine to keep awake.</li>
</ul>
</details>
<details>
<summary>Do I need to pay for an AI subscription separately?</summary>
<p dir="auto">Yes. CloudCLI provides the environment, not the AI. You bring your own Claude, Cursor, Codex, or Gemini subscription. CloudCLI Cloud starts at $7/month for the hosted environment on top of that.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary>Can I use CloudCLI UI on my phone?</summary>
<p dir="auto">Yes. For self-hosted, run the server on your machine and open <code>[yourip]:port</code> in any browser on your network. For CloudCLI Cloud, open it from any device — no VPN, no port forwarding, no setup. A native app is also in the works.</p>
</details>
<details>
<summary>Will changes I make in the UI affect my local Claude Code setup?</summary>
<p dir="auto">Yes, for self-hosted. CloudCLI UI reads from and writes to the same <code>~/.claude</code> config that Claude Code uses natively. MCP servers you add via the UI show up in Claude Code immediately and vice versa.</p>
</details>
<hr>
